Title: Cartas do P. Antonio Vieyra da Companhia de Jesu; tomo primeiro
Creator: António Vieira, S.J.; the collection begun by the Conde da Ericeira (dedication and prologue of tomo 1)
Publisher: Lisboa Occidental: na Officina da Congregação do Oratorio (title page)
Publication Date: 1735 (title page: 'M.DCC.XXXV.')
Rights: Public Domain (pd-us-by-date: published 1735); the archive.org record carries no rights field, so the imprint date is the basis
Language: por
Source: archive.org item cartasdopantonio00viei, full text from the item's own djvu.txt. Fetched directly 2026-11-15 by the Library thread; the byte count of the downloaded file (574,877) equals the size archive.org lists for the djvu.txt.
Provenance note: Extracted unaltered: the item's own djvu.txt is reproduced whole after this header; nothing was stripped, normalized or corrected. Rights basis: published 1735, out of US copyright by date. OCR quality: clean Portuguese with the long s read as f. Word count of the host text: 88,664. Line numbers cited in this header are lines of the host OCR; add 12 for this file's own line numbers.
Content note: Tomo 1. A library plate at OCR lines 7-12; title page at 38-72. Dedication, signed by the Conde da Ericeira, at 78-132; prologue at 141-322, which says the Conde began the collection; licences (1734-1735) at 323-870. Carta I at 873 (dated September 1647 at 919) to Carta CXXXXI at 16243 (Rome, 3 June 1674); 'Fim do Primeiro Tomo' at 16398. All the letters are Vieira's; none addressed to him was found. No index was found.
